+    cudaGraph_t graph_{};
+    cudaGraphExec_t executable_{};
+};
+
+constexpr int THREADS_PER_BLOCK = 256;
+constexpr int WARP_SIZE = 32;
+constexpr int WARPS_PER_BLOCK = THREADS_PER_BLOCK / WARP_SIZE;
+
+// Power-of-two cooperative groups avoid wasting a full warp on short rows.
+// All groups in a hardware warp execute the same shuffle instructions, so a
+// full active mask is valid while the width argument keeps reductions separate.
+template <int THREADS_PER_ROW>
+__global__ void spmvVectorKernel(const double* __restrict__ val,
+                                 const index_t* __restrict__ cols,
+                                 const index_t* __restrict__ rowDelimiters,
+                                 const double* __restrict__ vec,
+                                 const index_t dim,
+                                 double* __restrict__ out) {
+    const int lane = threadIdx.x & (THREADS_PER_ROW - 1);
+    index_t row = (blockIdx.x * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x) / THREADS_PER_ROW;
+    const index_t rowStride = (gridDim.x * blockDim.x) / THREADS_PER_ROW;
+
+    for (; row < dim; row += rowStride) {
+        double sum = 0.0;
+        const index_t rowStart = rowDelimiters[row];
+        const index_t rowEnd = rowDelimiters[row + 1];
+        for (index_t element = rowStart + lane; element < rowEnd; element += THREADS_PER_ROW) {
+            sum += val[element] * vec[cols[element]];
+        }
+
+#pragma unroll
+        for (int offset = THREADS_PER_ROW / 2; offset > 0; offset /= 2) {
+            sum += __shfl_down_sync(0xffffffffu, sum, offset, THREADS_PER_ROW);
+        }
+        if (lane == 0) {
+            out[row] = sum;
+        }
+    }
+}
+
+// Very long rows need more parallelism than a single warp can expose. This
+// kernel uses a full block per row and only eight shared-memory values during
+// its two-level reduction.
+__global__ void spmvBlockKernel(const double* __restrict__ val,
+                                const index_t* __restrict__ cols,
+                                const index_t* __restrict__ rowDelimiters,
+                                const double* __restrict__ vec,
+                                const index_t dim,
+                                double* __restrict__ out) {
+    __shared__ double warpSums[WARPS_PER_BLOCK];
+    const int lane = threadIdx.x & (WARP_SIZE - 1);
+    const int warp = threadIdx.x / WARP_SIZE;
+
+    for (index_t row = blockIdx.x; row < dim; row += gridDim.x) {
+        double sum = 0.0;
+        const index_t rowEnd = rowDelimiters[row + 1];
+        for (index_t element = rowDelimiters[row] + threadIdx.x;
+             element < rowEnd;
+             element += blockDim.x) {
+            sum += val[element] * vec[cols[element]];
+        }
+
+#pragma unroll
+        for (int offset = WARP_SIZE / 2; offset > 0; offset /= 2) {
+            sum += __shfl_down_sync(0xffffffffu, sum, offset);
+        }
+        if (lane == 0) {
+            warpSums[warp] = sum;
+        }
+        __syncthreads();
+
+        if (warp == 0) {
+            sum = lane < WARPS_PER_BLOCK ? warpSums[lane] : 0.0;
+#pragma unroll
+            for (int offset = WARP_SIZE / 2; offset > 0; offset /= 2) {
+                sum += __shfl_down_sync(0xffffffffu, sum, offset);
+            }
+            if (lane == 0) {
+                out[row] = sum;
+            }
+        }
+        __syncthreads();
+    }
+}
+
+enum class SpmvKernel { Vector2, Vector4, Vector8, Vector16, Vector32, Block };
+
+SpmvKernel chooseKernel(const index_t dim, const index_t nonzeros) {
+    const double elementsPerRow = dim == 0 ? 0.0 : static_cast<double>(nonzeros) / dim;
+    if (elementsPerRow <= 2.0) {
+        return SpmvKernel::Vector2;
+    }
+    if (elementsPerRow <= 4.0) {
+        return SpmvKernel::Vector4;
+    }
+    if (elementsPerRow <= 8.0) {
+        return SpmvKernel::Vector8;
+    }
+    if (elementsPerRow <= 16.0) {
+        return SpmvKernel::Vector16;
+    }
+    if (elementsPerRow <= 4096.0) {
+        return SpmvKernel::Vector32;
+    }
+    return SpmvKernel::Block;
+}
+
+void launchSpmv(const SpmvKernel kernel,
+                const double* const val,
+                const index_t* const cols,
+                const index_t* const rowDelimiters,
+                const double* const vec,
+                const index_t dim,
+                double* const out,
+                const int maxBlocks,
+                const cudaStream_t stream) {
+    if (dim == 0) {
+        return;
+    }
+
+    switch (kernel) {
+#define LAUNCH_VECTOR_KERNEL(vectorSize)                                                            \
+    do {                                                                                             \
+        constexpr int rowsPerBlock = THREADS_PER_BLOCK / (vectorSize);                              \
+        const int rowBlocks = static_cast<int>((static_cast<uint64_t>(dim) + rowsPerBlock - 1) /    \
+                                               rowsPerBlock);                                        \
+        spmvVectorKernel<vectorSize>                                                                 \
+            <<<rowBlocks < maxBlocks ? rowBlocks : maxBlocks, THREADS_PER_BLOCK, 0, stream>>>(       \
+                val, cols, rowDelimiters, vec, dim, out);                                            \
+    } while (false)
+        case SpmvKernel::Vector2:
+            LAUNCH_VECTOR_KERNEL(2);
+            break;
+        case SpmvKernel::Vector4:
+            LAUNCH_VECTOR_KERNEL(4);
+            break;
+        case SpmvKernel::Vector8:
+            LAUNCH_VECTOR_KERNEL(8);
+            break;
+        case SpmvKernel::Vector16:
+            LAUNCH_VECTOR_KERNEL(16);
+            break;
+        case SpmvKernel::Vector32:
+            LAUNCH_VECTOR_KERNEL(32);
+            break;
+#undef LAUNCH_VECTOR_KERNEL
+        case SpmvKernel::Block: {
+            const int rowBlocks = dim < static_cast<index_t>(maxBlocks) ? static_cast<int>(dim) : maxBlocks;
+            spmvBlockKernel<<<rowBlocks, THREADS_PER_BLOCK, 0, stream>>>(
+                val, cols, rowDelimiters, vec, dim, out);
+            break;
+        }
+    }
+}
+

 //

+// Keep all CUDA error handling in one place so asynchronous launch failures are
+// reported at the operation which observes them instead of producing bad data.
+void checkCuda(const cudaError_t error, const char* const operation) {
+    if (error != cudaSuccess) {
+        fprintf(stderr, "CUDA error in %s: %s\n", operation, cudaGetErrorString(error));
+        std::exit(EXIT_FAILURE);
+    }

+constexpr int THREADS_PER_BLOCK = 256;
+constexpr int WARP_SIZE = 32;
+constexpr int WARPS_PER_BLOCK = THREADS_PER_BLOCK / WARP_SIZE;

+// Power-of-two cooperative groups avoid wasting a full warp on short rows.
+// All groups in a hardware warp execute the same shuffle instructions, so a
+// full active mask is valid while the width argument keeps reductions separate.
+template <int THREADS_PER_ROW>
+__global__ void spmvVectorKernel(const double* __restrict__ val,
+                                 const index_t* __restrict__ cols,
+                                 const index_t* __restrict__ rowDelimiters,
+                                 const double* __restrict__ vec,
+                                 const index_t dim,
+                                 double* __restrict__ out) {
+    const int lane = threadIdx.x & (THREADS_PER_ROW - 1);
+    index_t row = (blockIdx.x * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x) / THREADS_PER_ROW;
+    const index_t rowStride = (gridDim.x * blockDim.x) / THREADS_PER_ROW;
+
+    for (; row < dim; row += rowStride) {
+        double sum = 0.0;
+        const index_t rowStart = rowDelimiters[row];
+        const index_t rowEnd = rowDelimiters[row + 1];
+        for (index_t element = rowStart + lane; element < rowEnd; element += THREADS_PER_ROW) {
+            sum += val[element] * vec[cols[element]];
+        }
+
+#pragma unroll
+        for (int offset = THREADS_PER_ROW / 2; offset > 0; offset /= 2) {
+            sum += __shfl_down_sync(0xffffffffu, sum, offset, THREADS_PER_ROW);
+        }
+        if (lane == 0) {
+            out[row] = sum;
+        }
+    }
+}
+
+// Very long rows need more parallelism than a single warp can expose. This
+// kernel uses a full block per row and only eight shared-memory values during
+// its two-level reduction.
+__global__ void spmvBlockKernel(const double* __restrict__ val,
+                                const index_t* __restrict__ cols,
+                                const index_t* __restrict__ rowDelimiters,
+                                const double* __restrict__ vec,
+                                const index_t dim,
+                                double* __restrict__ out) {
+    __shared__ double warpSums[WARPS_PER_BLOCK];
+    const int lane = threadIdx.x & (WARP_SIZE - 1);
+    const int warp = threadIdx.x / WARP_SIZE;
+
+    for (index_t row = blockIdx.x; row < dim; row += gridDim.x) {
+        double sum = 0.0;
+        const index_t rowEnd = rowDelimiters[row + 1];
+        for (index_t element = rowDelimiters[row] + threadIdx.x;
+             element < rowEnd;
+             element += blockDim.x) {
+            sum += val[element] * vec[cols[element]];
+        }
+
+#pragma unroll
+        for (int offset = WARP_SIZE / 2; offset > 0; offset /= 2) {
+            sum += __shfl_down_sync(0xffffffffu, sum, offset);
+        }
+        if (lane == 0) {

+        }

+                sum += __shfl_down_sync(0xffffffffu, sum, offset);
+            }
+            if (lane == 0) {
+                out[row] = sum;
+            }
+        }
+        __syncthreads();
+    }
+}

+enum class SpmvKernel { Vector2, Vector4, Vector8, Vector16, Vector32, Block };
+
+SpmvKernel chooseKernel(const index_t dim, const index_t nonzeros) {
+    const double elementsPerRow = dim == 0 ? 0.0 : static_cast<double>(nonzeros) / dim;
+    if (elementsPerRow <= 2.0) {
+        return SpmvKernel::Vector2;
+    }
+    if (elementsPerRow <= 4.0) {
+        return SpmvKernel::Vector4;
+    }
+    if (elementsPerRow <= 8.0) {
+        return SpmvKernel::Vector8;
+    }
+    if (elementsPerRow <= 16.0) {
+        return SpmvKernel::Vector16;
+    }
+    if (elementsPerRow <= 4096.0) {
+        return SpmvKernel::Vector32;
+    }
+    return SpmvKernel::Block;
+}
+
+void launchSpmv(const SpmvKernel kernel,
+                const double* const val,
+                const index_t* const cols,
+                const index_t* const rowDelimiters,
+                const double* const vec,
+                const index_t dim,
+                double* const out,
+                const int maxBlocks,
+                const cudaStream_t stream) {
+    if (dim == 0) {
+        return;
+    }
+
+    switch (kernel) {
+#define LAUNCH_VECTOR_KERNEL(vectorSize)                                                            \
+    do {                                                                                             \
+        constexpr int rowsPerBlock = THREADS_PER_BLOCK / (vectorSize);                              \
+        const int rowBlocks = static_cast<int>((static_cast<uint64_t>(dim) + rowsPerBlock - 1) /    \
+                                               rowsPerBlock);                                        \
+        spmvVectorKernel<vectorSize>                                                                 \
+            <<<rowBlocks < maxBlocks ? rowBlocks : maxBlocks, THREADS_PER_BLOCK, 0, stream>>>(       \
+                val, cols, rowDelimiters, vec, dim, out);                                            \
+    } while (false)
+        case SpmvKernel::Vector2:
+            LAUNCH_VECTOR_KERNEL(2);
+            break;
+        case SpmvKernel::Vector4:
+            LAUNCH_VECTOR_KERNEL(4);
+            break;
+        case SpmvKernel::Vector8:
+            LAUNCH_VECTOR_KERNEL(8);
+            break;
+        case SpmvKernel::Vector16:
+            LAUNCH_VECTOR_KERNEL(16);
+            break;
+        case SpmvKernel::Vector32:
+            LAUNCH_VECTOR_KERNEL(32);
+            break;
+#undef LAUNCH_VECTOR_KERNEL
+        case SpmvKernel::Block: {
+            const int rowBlocks = dim < static_cast<index_t>(maxBlocks) ? static_cast<int>(dim) : maxBlocks;
+            spmvBlockKernel<<<rowBlocks, THREADS_PER_BLOCK, 0, stream>>>(
+                val, cols, rowDelimiters, vec, dim, out);
+            break;
+        }
+    }
+}

    18	// Keep all CUDA error handling in one place so asynchronous launch failures are
    19	// reported at the operation which observes them instead of producing bad data.
    20	void checkCuda(const cudaError_t error, const char* const operation) {
    21	    if (error != cudaSuccess) {
    22	        fprintf(stderr, "CUDA error in %s: %s\n", operation, cudaGetErrorString(error));
    23	        std::exit(EXIT_FAILURE);
    24	    }
    25	}

    30	class DeviceBuffer {
    31	  public:
    32	    explicit DeviceBuffer(const size_t count) : count_(count) {
    33	        if (count_ != 0) {
    34	            CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(reinterpret_cast<void**>(&data_), count_ * sizeof(T)));
    35	        }
    36	    }
    37	
    38	    ~DeviceBuffer() {
    39	        if (data_ != nullptr) {
    40	            cudaFree(data_);
    41	        }
    42	    }
    43	
    44	    DeviceBuffer(const DeviceBuffer&) = delete;
    45	    DeviceBuffer& operator=(const DeviceBuffer&) = delete;
    46	
    47	    T* data() { return data_; }
    48	    const T* data() const { return data_; }
    49	    size_t sizeBytes() const { return count_ * sizeof(T); }
    50	
    51	  private:
    52	    T* data_ = nullptr;
    53	    size_t count_ = 0;
    54	};

   107	constexpr int THREADS_PER_BLOCK = 256;
   108	constexpr int WARP_SIZE = 32;
   109	constexpr int WARPS_PER_BLOCK = THREADS_PER_BLOCK / WARP_SIZE;
   110	
   111	// Power-of-two cooperative groups avoid wasting a full warp on short rows.
   112	// All groups in a hardware warp execute the same shuffle instructions, so a
   113	// full active mask is valid while the width argument keeps reductions separate.
   114	template <int THREADS_PER_ROW>
   115	__global__ void spmvVectorKernel(const double* __restrict__ val,
   116	                                 const index_t* __restrict__ cols,
   117	                                 const index_t* __restrict__ rowDelimiters,
   118	                                 const double* __restrict__ vec,
   119	                                 const index_t dim,
   120	                                 double* __restrict__ out) {
   121	    const int lane = threadIdx.x & (THREADS_PER_ROW - 1);
   122	    index_t row = (blockIdx.x * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x) / THREADS_PER_ROW;
   123	    const index_t rowStride = (gridDim.x * blockDim.x) / THREADS_PER_ROW;
   124	
   125	    for (; row < dim; row += rowStride) {
   126	        double sum = 0.0;
   127	        const index_t rowStart = rowDelimiters[row];
   128	        const index_t rowEnd = rowDelimiters[row + 1];
   129	        for (index_t element = rowStart + lane; element < rowEnd; element += THREADS_PER_ROW) {
   130	            sum += val[element] * vec[cols[element]];
   131	        }
   132	
   133	#pragma unroll
   134	        for (int offset = THREADS_PER_ROW / 2; offset > 0; offset /= 2) {
   135	            sum += __shfl_down_sync(0xffffffffu, sum, offset, THREADS_PER_ROW);
   136	        }
   137	        if (lane == 0) {
   138	            out[row] = sum;
   139	        }
   140	    }
   141	}
   142	
   143	// Very long rows need more parallelism than a single warp can expose. This
   144	// kernel uses a full block per row and only eight shared-memory values during
   145	// its two-level reduction.
   146	__global__ void spmvBlockKernel(const double* __restrict__ val,
   147	                                const index_t* __restrict__ cols,
   148	                                const index_t* __restrict__ rowDelimiters,
   149	                                const double* __restrict__ vec,
   150	                                const index_t dim,
   151	                                double* __restrict__ out) {
   152	    __shared__ double warpSums[WARPS_PER_BLOCK];
   153	    const int lane = threadIdx.x & (WARP_SIZE - 1);
   154	    const int warp = threadIdx.x / WARP_SIZE;
   155	
   156	    for (index_t row = blockIdx.x; row < dim; row += gridDim.x) {
   157	        double sum = 0.0;
   158	        const index_t rowEnd = rowDelimiters[row + 1];
   159	        for (index_t element = rowDelimiters[row] + threadIdx.x;
   160	             element < rowEnd;
   161	             element += blockDim.x) {
   162	            sum += val[element] * vec[cols[element]];
   163	        }
   164	
   165	#pragma unroll
   166	        for (int offset = WARP_SIZE / 2; offset > 0; offset /= 2) {
   167	            sum += __shfl_down_sync(0xffffffffu, sum, offset);
   168	        }
   169	        if (lane == 0) {
   170	            warpSums[warp] = sum;
   171	        }
   172	        __syncthreads();
   173	
   174	        if (warp == 0) {
   175	            sum = lane < WARPS_PER_BLOCK ? warpSums[lane] : 0.0;
   176	#pragma unroll
   177	            for (int offset = WARP_SIZE / 2; offset > 0; offset /= 2) {
   178	                sum += __shfl_down_sync(0xffffffffu, sum, offset);
   179	            }
   180	            if (lane == 0) {
   181	                out[row] = sum;
   182	            }
   183	        }
   184	        __syncthreads();
   185	    }
   186	}
   187	
   188	enum class SpmvKernel { Vector2, Vector4, Vector8, Vector16, Vector32, Block };
   189	
   190	SpmvKernel chooseKernel(const index_t dim, const index_t nonzeros) {
   191	    const double elementsPerRow = dim == 0 ? 0.0 : static_cast<double>(nonzeros) / dim;
   192	    if (elementsPerRow <= 2.0) {
   193	        return SpmvKernel::Vector2;
   194	    }
   195	    if (elementsPerRow <= 4.0) {
   196	        return SpmvKernel::Vector4;
   197	    }
   198	    if (elementsPerRow <= 8.0) {
   199	        return SpmvKernel::Vector8;
   200	    }
   201	    if (elementsPerRow <= 16.0) {
   202	        return SpmvKernel::Vector16;
   203	    }
   204	    if (elementsPerRow <= 4096.0) {
   205	        return SpmvKernel::Vector32;
   206	    }
   207	    return SpmvKernel::Block;
   208	}
   209	
   210	void launchSpmv(const SpmvKernel kernel,
   211	                const double* const val,
   212	                const index_t* const cols,
   213	                const index_t* const rowDelimiters,
   214	                const double* const vec,
   215	                const index_t dim,
   216	                double* const out,
   217	                const int maxBlocks,
   218	                const cudaStream_t stream) {
   219	    if (dim == 0) {
   220	        return;
   221	    }
   222	
   223	    switch (kernel) {
   224	#define LAUNCH_VECTOR_KERNEL(vectorSize)                                                            \
   225	    do {                                                                                             \
   226	        constexpr int rowsPerBlock = THREADS_PER_BLOCK / (vectorSize);                              \
   227	        const int rowBlocks = static_cast<int>((static_cast<uint64_t>(dim) + rowsPerBlock - 1) /    \
   228	                                               rowsPerBlock);                                        \
   229	        spmvVectorKernel<vectorSize>                                                                 \
   230	            <<<rowBlocks < maxBlocks ? rowBlocks : maxBlocks, THREADS_PER_BLOCK, 0, stream>>>(       \
   390	    printf("  -n <num>     Number of rows/columns in the matrix (default: 1024)\n");
   391	    printf("  -s <num>     Sparsity: 1 out of N entries is non-zero (default: 10)\n");
   392	    printf("  -i <num>     Number of iterations (default: 10)\n");
   393	    printf("  -m <val>     Maximum value for matrix and vector elements (default: 1.0)\n");
   394	    printf("  -v           Enable validation\n");
   395	    printf("  -r           Print results for external validation\n");
   396	    printf("  -h           Show this help message\n");
   397	}

   486	        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(d_cols.data(), h_cols.data(), d_cols.sizeBytes(), cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));
   487	    }
   488	    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(d_rowDelimiters.data(), h_rowDelimiters.data(),
   489	                          d_rowDelimiters.sizeBytes(), cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));
   490	    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(d_vec.data(), h_vec.data(), d_vec.sizeBytes(), cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));
   491	
   492	    cudaDeviceProp deviceProperties{};
   493	    int device = 0;
   494	    CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetDevice(&device));
   495	    CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetDeviceProperties(&deviceProperties, device));
   496	    const int maxBlocks = deviceProperties.multiProcessorCount * 8;
   497	    const SpmvKernel kernel = chooseKernel(numRows, nItems);
   498	    CudaStream computeStream;
   499	
   500	    // Warm up the CUDA context and selected kernel so one-time startup work is
   501	    // not charged to the SpMV iterations.
   502	    launchSpmv(kernel, d_val.data(), d_cols.data(), d_rowDelimiters.data(),
   503	               d_vec.data(), numRows, d_out.data(), maxBlocks, computeStream.get());
   504	    CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetLastError());
   505	    CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamSynchronize(computeStream.get()));
   506

   508	    // Graph eliminates host launch gaps without changing the work performed.
   509	    CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamBeginCapture(computeStream.get(), cudaStreamCaptureModeGlobal));
   510	    for (index_t iter = 0; iter < iterations; ++iter) {
   511	        launchSpmv(kernel, d_val.data(), d_cols.data(), d_rowDelimiters.data(),
   512	                   d_vec.data(), numRows, d_out.data(), maxBlocks, computeStream.get());
   513	    }
   514	    cudaGraph_t capturedGraph = nullptr;
   515	    CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamEndCapture(computeStream.get(), &capturedGraph));
   516	    CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetLastError());
   517	    const CudaGraph iterationGraph(capturedGraph);
   518	
   519	    // The first graph launch performs lazy driver setup; keep it out of the
   520	    // measurement just like context and kernel warm-up.
   521	    iterationGraph.launch(computeStream.get());
   522	    CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamSynchronize(computeStream.get()));
   523	
   524	    // Perform SpMV computation
   525	    printf("Computing SpMV...\n");
   526	    CudaEvent start;
   527	    CudaEvent end;
   528	    CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventRecord(start.get(), computeStream.get()));
   529	    iterationGraph.launch(computeStream.get());
   530	    CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventRecord(end.get(), computeStream.get()));
   531	    CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetLastError());

   533	    float durationMs = 0.0f;

 // Constants
 constexpr double MAX_RELATIVE_ERROR = 0.02;
 
+// Keep all CUDA error handling in one place so asynchronous launch failures are
+// reported at the operation which observes them instead of producing bad data.
+void checkCuda(const cudaError_t error, const char* const operation) {
+    if (error != cudaSuccess) {
+        fprintf(stderr, "CUDA error in %s: %s\n", operation, cudaGetErrorString(error));
+        std::exit(EXIT_FAILURE);
+    }
+}
+
+#define CUDA_CHECK(operation) checkCuda((operation), #operation)
+
+template <typename T>
+class DeviceBuffer {
+  public:
+    explicit DeviceBuffer(const size_t count) : count_(count) {
+        if (count_ != 0) {
+            CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(reinterpret_cast<void**>(&data_), count_ * sizeof(T)));
+        }
+    }
+
+    ~DeviceBuffer() {
+        if (data_ != nullptr) {
+            cudaFree(data_);
+        }
+    }
+
+    DeviceBuffer(const DeviceBuffer&) = delete;
+    DeviceBuffer& operator=(const DeviceBuffer&) = delete;
+
+    T* data() { return data_; }
+    const T* data() const { return data_; }
+    size_t sizeBytes() const { return count_ * sizeof(T); }
+
+  private:
+    T* data_ = nullptr;
+    size_t count_ = 0;
+};
+
+class CudaEvent {
+  public:
+    CudaEvent() { CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventCreate(&event_)); }
+    ~CudaEvent() { cudaEventDestroy(event_); }
+
+    CudaEvent(const CudaEvent&) = delete;
+    CudaEvent& operator=(const CudaEvent&) = delete;
+
+    cudaEvent_t get() const { return event_; }
+
+  private:
+    cudaEvent_t event_{};
+};
+
+class CudaStream {
+  public:
+    CudaStream() { CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamCreateWithFlags(&stream_, cudaStreamNonBlocking)); }
+    ~CudaStream() { cudaStreamDestroy(stream_); }
+
+    CudaStream(const CudaStream&) = delete;
+    CudaStream& operator=(const CudaStream&) = delete;
+
+    cudaStream_t get() const { return stream_; }
+
+  private:
+    cudaStream_t stream_{};
+};
+
+class CudaGraph {
+  public:
+    explicit CudaGraph(cudaGraph_t graph) : graph_(graph) {
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaGraphInstantiate(&executable_, graph_, nullptr, nullptr, 0));
+    }
+
+    ~CudaGraph() {
+        cudaGraphExecDestroy(executable_);
+        cudaGraphDestroy(graph_);
+    }
+
+    CudaGraph(const CudaGraph&) = delete;
+    CudaGraph& operator=(const CudaGraph&) = delete;
+
+    void launch(const cudaStream_t stream) const {
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaGraphLaunch(executable_, stream));
+    }
+
+  private:
+    cudaGraph_t graph_{};
+    cudaGraphExec_t executable_{};
+};

+constexpr int THREADS_PER_BLOCK = 256;
+constexpr int WARP_SIZE = 32;
+constexpr int WARPS_PER_BLOCK = THREADS_PER_BLOCK / WARP_SIZE;
+
+// Power-of-two cooperative groups avoid wasting a full warp on short rows.
+// All groups in a hardware warp execute the same shuffle instructions, so a
+// full active mask is valid while the width argument keeps reductions separate.
+template <int THREADS_PER_ROW>
+__global__ void spmvVectorKernel(const double* __restrict__ val,
+                                 const index_t* __restrict__ cols,
+                                 const index_t* __restrict__ rowDelimiters,
+                                 const double* __restrict__ vec,
+                                 const index_t dim,
+                                 double* __restrict__ out) {
+    const int lane = threadIdx.x & (THREADS_PER_ROW - 1);
+    index_t row = (blockIdx.x * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x) / THREADS_PER_ROW;
+    const index_t rowStride = (gridDim.x * blockDim.x) / THREADS_PER_ROW;
+
+    for (; row < dim; row += rowStride) {
+        double sum = 0.0;
+        const index_t rowStart = rowDelimiters[row];
+        const index_t rowEnd = rowDelimiters[row + 1];
+        for (index_t element = rowStart + lane; element < rowEnd; element += THREADS_PER_ROW) {
+            sum += val[element] * vec[cols[element]];
+        }
+
+#pragma unroll
+        for (int offset = THREADS_PER_ROW / 2; offset > 0; offset /= 2) {
+            sum += __shfl_down_sync(0xffffffffu, sum, offset, THREADS_PER_ROW);
+        }
+        if (lane == 0) {
+            out[row] = sum;
+        }
+    }
+}
+
+// Very long rows need more parallelism than a single warp can expose. This
+// kernel uses a full block per row and only eight shared-memory values during
+// its two-level reduction.
+__global__ void spmvBlockKernel(const double* __restrict__ val,
+                                const index_t* __restrict__ cols,
+                                const index_t* __restrict__ rowDelimiters,
+                                const double* __restrict__ vec,
+                                const index_t dim,
+                                double* __restrict__ out) {
+    __shared__ double warpSums[WARPS_PER_BLOCK];
+    const int lane = threadIdx.x & (WARP_SIZE - 1);
+    const int warp = threadIdx.x / WARP_SIZE;
+
+    for (index_t row = blockIdx.x; row < dim; row += gridDim.x) {
+        double sum = 0.0;
+        const index_t rowEnd = rowDelimiters[row + 1];
+        for (index_t element = rowDelimiters[row] + threadIdx.x;
+             element < rowEnd;
+             element += blockDim.x) {
+            sum += val[element] * vec[cols[element]];
+        }
+
+#pragma unroll
+        for (int offset = WARP_SIZE / 2; offset > 0; offset /= 2) {
+            sum += __shfl_down_sync(0xffffffffu, sum, offset);
+        }
+        if (lane == 0) {
+            warpSums[warp] = sum;
+        }
+        __syncthreads();
+
+        if (warp == 0) {
+            sum = lane < WARPS_PER_BLOCK ? warpSums[lane] : 0.0;
+#pragma unroll
+            for (int offset = WARP_SIZE / 2; offset > 0; offset /= 2) {
+                sum += __shfl_down_sync(0xffffffffu, sum, offset);
+            }
+            if (lane == 0) {
+                out[row] = sum;
+            }
+        }
+        __syncthreads();
+    }
+}
+
+enum class SpmvKernel { Vector2, Vector4, Vector8, Vector16, Vector32, Block };
+
+SpmvKernel chooseKernel(const index_t dim, const index_t nonzeros) {
+    const double elementsPerRow = dim == 0 ? 0.0 : static_cast<double>(nonzeros) / dim;
+    if (elementsPerRow <= 2.0) {
+        return SpmvKernel::Vector2;
+    }
+    if (elementsPerRow <= 4.0) {
+        return SpmvKernel::Vector4;
+    }
+    if (elementsPerRow <= 8.0) {
+        return SpmvKernel::Vector8;
+    }
+    if (elementsPerRow <= 16.0) {
+        return SpmvKernel::Vector16;
+    }
+    if (elementsPerRow <= 4096.0) {
+        return SpmvKernel::Vector32;
+    }
+    return SpmvKernel::Block;
+}
+
+void launchSpmv(const SpmvKernel kernel,
+                const double* const val,
+                const index_t* const cols,
+                const index_t* const rowDelimiters,
+                const double* const vec,
+                const index_t dim,
+                double* const out,
+                const int maxBlocks,
+                const cudaStream_t stream) {
+    if (dim == 0) {
+        return;
+    }
+
+    switch (kernel) {
+#define LAUNCH_VECTOR_KERNEL(vectorSize)                                                            \
+    do {                                                                                             \
+        constexpr int rowsPerBlock = THREADS_PER_BLOCK / (vectorSize);                              \
+        const int rowBlocks = static_cast<int>((static_cast<uint64_t>(dim) + rowsPerBlock - 1) /    \
+                                               rowsPerBlock);                                        \
+        spmvVectorKernel<vectorSize>                                                                 \
+            <<<rowBlocks < maxBlocks ? rowBlocks : maxBlocks, THREADS_PER_BLOCK, 0, stream>>>(       \
+                val, cols, rowDelimiters, vec, dim, out);                                            \
+    } while (false)
+        case SpmvKernel::Vector2:
+            LAUNCH_VECTOR_KERNEL(2);
+            break;
+        case SpmvKernel::Vector4:
+            LAUNCH_VECTOR_KERNEL(4);
+            break;
+        case SpmvKernel::Vector8:
+            LAUNCH_VECTOR_KERNEL(8);
+            break;
+        case SpmvKernel::Vector16:
+            LAUNCH_VECTOR_KERNEL(16);
+            break;
+        case SpmvKernel::Vector32:
+            LAUNCH_VECTOR_KERNEL(32);
+            break;
+#undef LAUNCH_VECTOR_KERNEL
+        case SpmvKernel::Block: {
+            const int rowBlocks = dim < static_cast<index_t>(maxBlocks) ? static_cast<int>(dim) : maxBlocks;
+            spmvBlockKernel<<<rowBlocks, THREADS_PER_BLOCK, 0, stream>>>(
+                val, cols, rowDelimiters, vec, dim, out);
+            break;
+        }
+    }
+}
+

 // Constants
 constexpr double MAX_RELATIVE_ERROR = 0.02;
 
+// Keep all CUDA error handling in one place so asynchronous launch failures are
+// reported at the operation which observes them instead of producing bad data.
+void checkCuda(const cudaError_t error, const char* const operation) {
+    if (error != cudaSuccess) {
+        fprintf(stderr, "CUDA error in %s: %s\n", operation, cudaGetErrorString(error));
+        std::exit(EXIT_FAILURE);
+    }
+}
+
+#define CUDA_CHECK(operation) checkCuda((operation), #operation)
+
+template <typename T>
+class DeviceBuffer {
+  public:
+    explicit DeviceBuffer(const size_t count) : count_(count) {
+        if (count_ != 0) {
+            CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(reinterpret_cast<void**>(&data_), count_ * sizeof(T)));
+        }
+    }
+
+    ~DeviceBuffer() {
+        if (data_ != nullptr) {
+            cudaFree(data_);
+        }
+    }
+
+    DeviceBuffer(const DeviceBuffer&) = delete;
+    DeviceBuffer& operator=(const DeviceBuffer&) = delete;
+
+    T* data() { return data_; }
+    const T* data() const { return data_; }
+    size_t sizeBytes() const { return count_ * sizeof(T); }
+
+  private:
+    T* data_ = nullptr;
+    size_t count_ = 0;
+};
+

+class CudaGraph {

+    }

+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaGraphLaunch(executable_, stream));
+    }

+    cudaGraphExec_t executable_{};

+constexpr int THREADS_PER_BLOCK = 256;
+constexpr int WARP_SIZE = 32;
+constexpr int WARPS_PER_BLOCK = THREADS_PER_BLOCK / WARP_SIZE;
+
+// Power-of-two cooperative groups avoid wasting a full warp on short rows.
+// All groups in a hardware warp execute the same shuffle instructions, so a
+// full active mask is valid while the width argument keeps reductions separate.
+template <int THREADS_PER_ROW>
+__global__ void spmvVectorKernel(const double* __restrict__ val,
+                                 const index_t* __restrict__ cols,
+                                 const index_t* __restrict__ rowDelimiters,
+                                 const double* __restrict__ vec,
+                                 const index_t dim,
+                                 double* __restrict__ out) {
+    const int lane = threadIdx.x & (THREADS_PER_ROW - 1);
+    index_t row = (blockIdx.x * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x) / THREADS_PER_ROW;
+    const index_t rowStride = (gridDim.x * blockDim.x) / THREADS_PER_ROW;
+
+    for (; row < dim; row += rowStride) {
+        double sum = 0.0;
+        const index_t rowStart = rowDelimiters[row];
+        const index_t rowEnd = rowDelimiters[row + 1];
+        for (index_t element = rowStart + lane; element < rowEnd; element += THREADS_PER_ROW) {
+            sum += val[element] * vec[cols[element]];
+        }
+
+#pragma unroll
+        for (int offset = THREADS_PER_ROW / 2; offset > 0; offset /= 2) {
+            sum += __shfl_down_sync(0xffffffffu, sum, offset, THREADS_PER_ROW);
+        }
+        if (lane == 0) {
+            out[row] = sum;
+        }
+    }
+}
+
+// Very long rows need more parallelism than a single warp can expose. This
+// kernel uses a full block per row and only eight shared-memory values during
+// its two-level reduction.
+__global__ void spmvBlockKernel(const double* __restrict__ val,
+                                const index_t* __restrict__ cols,
+                                const index_t* __restrict__ rowDelimiters,
+                                const double* __restrict__ vec,
+                                const index_t dim,
+                                double* __restrict__ out) {
+    __shared__ double warpSums[WARPS_PER_BLOCK];
+    const int lane = threadIdx.x & (WARP_SIZE - 1);
+    const int warp = threadIdx.x / WARP_SIZE;
+
+    for (index_t row = blockIdx.x; row < dim; row += gridDim.x) {
+        double sum = 0.0;
+        const index_t rowEnd = rowDelimiters[row + 1];
+        for (index_t element = rowDelimiters[row] + threadIdx.x;
+             element < rowEnd;
+             element += blockDim.x) {
+            sum += val[element] * vec[cols[element]];
+        }
+
+#pragma unroll
+        for (int offset = WARP_SIZE / 2; offset > 0; offset /= 2) {
+            sum += __shfl_down_sync(0xffffffffu, sum, offset);
+        }
+        if (lane == 0) {
+            warpSums[warp] = sum;
+        }
+        __syncthreads();
+
+        if (warp == 0) {
+            sum = lane < WARPS_PER_BLOCK ? warpSums[lane] : 0.0;
+#pragma unroll
+            for (int offset = WARP_SIZE / 2; offset > 0; offset /= 2) {
+                sum += __shfl_down_sync(0xffffffffu, sum, offset);
+            }
+            if (lane == 0) {
+                out[row] = sum;
+            }
+        }
+        __syncthreads();
+    }
+}
+
+enum class SpmvKernel { Vector2, Vector4, Vector8, Vector16, Vector32, Block };
+
+SpmvKernel chooseKernel(const index_t dim, const index_t nonzeros) {
+    const double elementsPerRow = dim == 0 ? 0.0 : static_cast<double>(nonzeros) / dim;
+    if (elementsPerRow <= 2.0) {
+        return SpmvKernel::Vector2;
+    }
+    if (elementsPerRow <= 4.0) {
+        return SpmvKernel::Vector4;
+    }
+    if (elementsPerRow <= 8.0) {
+        return SpmvKernel::Vector8;
+    }
+    if (elementsPerRow <= 16.0) {
+        return SpmvKernel::Vector16;
+    }
+    if (elementsPerRow <= 4096.0) {
+        return SpmvKernel::Vector32;
+    }
+    return SpmvKernel::Block;
+}
+
+void launchSpmv(const SpmvKernel kernel,
+                const double* const val,
+                const index_t* const cols,
+                const index_t* const rowDelimiters,
+                const double* const vec,
+                const index_t dim,
+                double* const out,
+                const int maxBlocks,
+                const cudaStream_t stream) {
+    if (dim == 0) {
+        return;
+    }
+
+    switch (kernel) {
+#define LAUNCH_VECTOR_KERNEL(vectorSize)                                                            \
+    do {                                                                                             \
+        constexpr int rowsPerBlock = THREADS_PER_BLOCK / (vectorSize);                              \
+        const int rowBlocks = static_cast<int>((static_cast<uint64_t>(dim) + rowsPerBlock - 1) /    \
+                                               rowsPerBlock);                                        \
+        spmvVectorKernel<vectorSize>                                                                 \
+            <<<rowBlocks < maxBlocks ? rowBlocks : maxBlocks, THREADS_PER_BLOCK, 0, stream>>>(       \
+                val, cols, rowDelimiters, vec, dim, out);                                            \
+    } while (false)
+        case SpmvKernel::Vector2:
+            LAUNCH_VECTOR_KERNEL(2);
+            break;
+        case SpmvKernel::Vector4:
+            LAUNCH_VECTOR_KERNEL(4);
+            break;
+        case SpmvKernel::Vector8:
+            LAUNCH_VECTOR_KERNEL(8);
+            break;
+        case SpmvKernel::Vector16:
+            LAUNCH_VECTOR_KERNEL(16);
+            break;
+        case SpmvKernel::Vector32:
+            LAUNCH_VECTOR_KERNEL(32);
+            break;
+#undef LAUNCH_VECTOR_KERNEL
+        case SpmvKernel::Block: {
+            const int rowBlocks = dim < static_cast<index_t>(maxBlocks) ? static_cast<int>(dim) : maxBlocks;
+            spmvBlockKernel<<<rowBlocks, THREADS_PER_BLOCK, 0, stream>>>(
+                val, cols, rowDelimiters, vec, dim, out);
+            break;
+        }
+    }
+}
+
